<!-- em:section id="model-i18n.context.context_domain_FederationLearningPlatform_context_FileUpload" sources="domain/FederationLearningPlatform/context/FileUpload domain/FederationLearningPlatform/context/FileUpload/concept/UploadedFile" -->
## 上下文术语: File Upload

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Available | 可用 | 已翻译 |
| Business references can become invalid if retention expires a file that is still referenced by a domain record. | 如果保留期导致仍被域记录引用的文件过期，则业务引用可能失效。 | 已翻译 |
| Discard File | 丢弃文件 | 已翻译 |
| Discarded | 已放弃 | 已翻译 |
| Download File | 下载文件 | 已翻译 |
| Expire File | 使文件过期 | 已翻译 |
| Expired | 已过期 | 已翻译 |
| File Upload | 文件上传 | 已翻译 |
| FileUpload is the shared persistent file-storage boundary. It stores uploaded content through a replaceable storage adapter, records each uploaded file, exposes an authenticated content URI, and applies optional retention through expiresAt. Business domains reference the uploaded file record instead of creating another physical storage tier. | FileUpload 是共享的持久化文件存储边界。它通过可替换的存储适配器存储已上传内容，记录每个已上传文件，公开经过身份验证的内容 URI，并通过 expiresAt 应用可选的保留期。业务域引用已上传文件记录，而不是再创建一个物理存储层。 | 已翻译 |
| fileUploadFailureRate | fileUploadFailureRate | 已翻译 |
| fileUploadLatency | fileUploadLatency | 已翻译 |
| Mark File Referenced | 将文件标记为已引用 | 已翻译 |
| Referenced | 已引用 | 已翻译 |
| Stored files can leak sensitive model or dataset content if retention, ownership, authorization, and content validation are not enforced. | 如果未强制实施保留期、所有权、授权和内容验证，则存储的文件可能泄漏敏感的模型或数据集内容。 | 已翻译 |
| Upload File | 上传文件 | 已翻译 |
| Uploaded File | 已上传文件 | 已翻译 |
| Uploaded File Catalog | 已上传文件目录 | 已翻译 |

<!-- em:section id="model-i18n.slice.slice_domain_FederationLearningPlatform_context_FileUpload_slice_UploadFile" source="domain/FederationLearningPlatform/context/FileUpload/slice/UploadFile" -->
## Slice 术语: Upload File

- Context: File Upload

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A file field uploads the selected content through the FileUpload deployment. The storage adapter persists the bytes in its configured backend, computes checksum metadata, assigns an authenticated content URI, and records the upload under fileId. Business domains keep the returned reference and own its business meaning. expiresAt is the retention policy for the same stored object. | 文件字段通过 FileUpload 部署上传所选内容。存储适配器在其配置的后端中持久化字节、计算校验和元数据、分配经过身份验证的内容 URI，并在 fileId 下记录此次上传。业务域保留返回的引用并拥有其业务含义。expiresAt 是同一存储对象的保留策略。 | 已翻译 |

| DownloadFile resolves an authenticated content URI through the configured FileUpload storage adapter. The command carries only fileId; original file metadata and downloadUri are resolved from UploadedFile state and the storage backend. File bytes are streamed by the download resource and are never copied into an Axon command or event. Referenced files remain downloadable, while discarded or expired files are rejected. | DownloadFile 通过已配置的 FileUpload 存储适配器解析经过身份验证的内容 URI。该命令仅携带 fileId；原始文件元数据和 downloadUri 由 UploadedFile 状态和存储后端解析得出。文件字节由下载资源以流式传输，绝不会被复制到 Axon 命令或事件中。被引用的文件保持可下载，而被丢弃或已过期的文件将被拒绝。 | 已翻译 |

| Runtime infrastructure verification runs once after the runtime installer confirms that the participant node has joined the target runtime infrastructure and the generated scheduling labels and taints have been applied. The verification processor resolves the selected runtime infrastructure package and records exactly one outcome: RuntimeInfrastructureVerified when the runtime environment is usable, or RuntimeInfrastructureVerificationFailed when required checks fail. | 运行时基础设施验证在运行时安装程序确认参与者节点已加入目标运行时基础设施并已应用生成的调度标签和污点后运行一次。验证处理器解析所选的运行时基础设施包，并仅记录一种结果：当运行时环境可用时为 RuntimeInfrastructureVerified（运行时基础设施已验证），或者当所需检查失败时为 RuntimeInfrastructureVerificationFailed（运行时基础设施验证失败）。 | 已翻译 |

The deployment service deploys the agent workload to the target infrastructure and records exactly one outcome: RuntimeAgentInstallationSucceeded when the agent starts and passes its readiness checks, or RuntimeAgentInstallationFailed when deployment or readiness fails. | 当所选安装模式为平台管理时，运行时基础设施验证通过后，运行时代理部署将自动运行。部署服务将代理工作负载部署到目标基础设施，并准确记录一项结果：代理启动并通过就绪性检查时为 RuntimeAgentInstallationSucceeded，部署或就绪性失败时为 RuntimeAgentInstallationFailed。 | 已翻译 |

## Slice 术语: Retry Runtime Infrastructure Verification

- Context: Runtime Provisioning

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |

| A platform admin can retry runtime infrastructure verification after fixing external runtime preparation problems such as missing Kubernetes node labels, unavailable nodes, registry access, or scheduler RBAC. The retry is allowed only from the VerificationFailed state and records exactly one new outcome without rewriting the original failed verification event. | 平台管理员可以在修复外部运行时准备问题（例如缺失 Kubernetes 节点标签、节点不可用、注册表访问或调度器 RBAC）后重试运行时基础设施验证。仅允许从 VerificationFailed（验证失败）状态进行重试，并且仅记录一个新结果，不会重写原始的失败验证事件。 | 已翻译 |

| The runtime installation plan screen exposes a retry deployment action only when the current runtime infrastructure state is RuntimeAgentFailed or Offline. A platform admin manually triggers the same platform-managed deployment port from the page; no human installs the agent on the server. The retry records exactly one outcome: RuntimeAgentDeploymentRetrySucceeded when the agent becomes ready, or RuntimeAgentDeploymentRetryFailed when deployment or readiness fails again. | 运行时安装计划屏幕仅在当前运行时基础设施状态为 RuntimeAgentFailed 或 Offline 时才会显示重试部署操作。平台管理员从该页面手动触发同一平台管理的部署端口；不会有人在服务器上手动安装代理。重试仅记录一个结果：代理就绪时为 RuntimeAgentDeploymentRetrySucceeded，或部署或就绪再次失败时为 RuntimeAgentDeploymentRetryFailed。 | 已翻译 |

## Slice 术语: Define Feature Schema

- Context: Dataset Governance

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A feature schema is uniquely identified by the combination of its domain and system-assigned version. Two schemas within the same domain cannot share a version, and users do not manually type the next version. The schema data modality describes whether the feature list represents tabular columns or logical inputs for unstructured data such as text, image, audio, document, or embedding datasets. | 特征架构通过其域与系统分配的版本的组合进行唯一标识。同一个域下的两个架构不能共享版本，用户不会手动输入下一个版本。架构的数据模态描述特征列表是表示表格列，还是文本、图像、音频、文档或嵌入数据集等非结构化数据的逻辑输入。 | 已翻译 |
| Count entries in the features collection. | 统计特征集合中的条目数量。 | 已翻译 |

| Registering a model artifact is a port-backed operation over a file already persisted by FileUpload. modelName is the human-readable artifact name shown to operators. modelPlugin is the strict runtime-engine model plugin code used to select DenseNet, ResNet, UNet, or other execution implementations. modelVersion identifies the business version under that display name, and modelDescription captures the business purpose, training background, applicable scenarios, or limitations that operators need when selecting the model. The pair must be unique. modelId remains the system identity for one imported or trained artifact version. sourceType distinguishes externally uploaded artifacts from artifacts produced by federated training. fileId identifies the shared FileUpload record; ModelRepository does not create a second physical copy or a second storage subsystem. The adapter resolves FileUpload metadata and content URI, validates the model format, records digest and size, and marks the file reference after registration. modelArtifactUri remains the authenticated FileUpload content URI.<br><br>unique (ModelArtifact.modelName, ModelArtifact.modelVersion) | 注册模型工件是一项由端口支持的操作，基于已由 FileUpload 持久化的文件。modelName 是向操作员展示的人工可读工件名称。modelPlugin 是严格的运行时引擎模型插件代码，用于选择 DenseNet、ResNet、UNet 或其他执行实现。modelVersion 标识该显示名称下的业务版本，modelDescription 捕获操作员在选择模型时所需的业务目的、训练背景、适用场景或限制。该对必须是唯一的。modelId 仍然是单个导入或训练的工件版本的系统标识。sourceType 区分外部上传的工件与联邦训练生成的工件。fileId 标识共享的 FileUpload 记录；ModelRepository 不会创建第二个物理副本或第二个存储子系统。适配器解析 FileUpload 元数据和内容 URI，验证模型格式，记录摘要和大小，并在注册后标记文件引用。modelArtifactUri 仍然是经过身份验证的 FileUpload 内容 URI。<br><br>唯一 (ModelArtifact.modelName, ModelArtifact.modelVersion) | 已翻译 |

| DownloadModelArtifact accepts only modelId. The adapter loads the registered ModelArtifact snapshot, verifies that its referenced FileUpload object is still available, and returns an authenticated downloadUri. ModelRepository owns model identity, version, format, and digest; FileUpload remains responsible for streaming the underlying bytes. No model content is carried in the command or event. | DownloadModelArtifact 仅接受 modelId。适配器加载已注册的 ModelArtifact 快照，验证其引用的 FileUpload 对象仍可用，并返回经过身份验证的 downloadUri。ModelRepository 拥有模型标识、版本、格式和摘要；FileUpload 仍负责流式传输底层字节。命令或事件中不携带任何模型内容。 | 已翻译 |

## Slice 术语: Register Federated Model Artifact

- Context: Model Repository

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A global model produced by the aggregation runtime is uploaded through the shared FileUpload storage boundary before GlobalModelUpdated is emitted. ModelArtifact registration records that file URI together with digest, format, size, producing training job, and round; it does not create a second storage area. The generated modelId is reused as both the upload record identifier and ModelArtifact identity for traceability. | 由聚合运行时生成的全局模型会在发出 GlobalModelUpdated 事件之前，通过共享的 FileUpload 存储边界上传。ModelArtifact 注册会记录该文件的 URI 以及摘要、格式、大小、生成训练作业和轮次；该注册不会创建第二个存储区域。生成的 modelId 会同时作为上传记录标识符和 ModelArtifact 标识进行复用，以实现可追溯性。 | 已翻译 |

| RuntimeEngineProfile is the business selectable runtime engine package used by training configurations. It hides raw image selection behind an operator-managed profile name while snapshotting the exact image, plugin profile, and optional digest that runtime agents must use when starting per-round runtime-engine workloads. Training runs must not depend on runtime-agent local environment defaults when a profile is selected.<br><br>unique RuntimeEngineProfile.profileName | RuntimeEngineProfile 是训练配置所使用的业务可选运行时引擎包。它将原始镜像选择隐藏在操作员管理的配置文件名称之后，同时对运行时代理在启动每轮运行时引擎工作负载时必须使用的精确镜像、插件配置文件和可选摘要进行快照。选择配置文件后，训练运行不得依赖于运行时代理本地环境的默认值。<br><br>unique RuntimeEngineProfile.profileName | 已翻译 |

## Slice 术语: Update Training Run Configuration

- Context: Training Orchestration

| A training run configuration can be updated only while it is still Draft. The updated values must satisfy the same runnable configuration rules as creation. After the configuration is locked by training submission, correction must happen by creating a new configuration. | 训练运行配置仅在其仍处于草稿状态时可更新。更新后的值必须满足与创建时相同的可运行配置规则。配置被训练提交锁定后，必须通过创建新配置进行更正。 | 已翻译 |

<!-- em:section id="model-i18n.slice.slice_domain_FederationLearningPlatform_context_TrainingOrchestration_slice_SelectTrainingRoundParticipants" source="domain/FederationLearningPlatform/context/TrainingOrchestration/slice/SelectTrainingRoundParticipants" -->
## Slice 术语: Select Training Round Participants

- Context: Training Orchestration

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Aggregation Algorithm | 聚合算法 | 已翻译 |
| Aggregation Algorithm is required | 聚合算法为必填项 | 已翻译 |
| Configuration Name | 配置名称 | 已翻译 |
| Configuration Name is required | 配置名称为必填项 | 已翻译 |
| Each training round starts from a runtime snapshot selected by domain policy. The policy evaluates submitted job, locked training configuration, active federation membership, active runtime identity, detected runtime capabilities, connected runtime status, and approved dataset evidence matching the feature schema. Runtimes that become ready after a round has begun cannot join that round, but they can be selected for a later round when policy allows dynamic round snapshots. If the selected runtime count is below quorum, the system records a failed participant selection event so the operator can fix runtime or dataset readiness and retry selection. | 每轮训练从域策略所选的运行时快照开始。该策略评估已提交的作业、已锁定的训练配置、活跃的联邦成员、活跃的运行时身份、检测到的运行时能力、已连接的运行时状态，以及与特征架构匹配的已批准数据集证据。在某轮训练开始后才就绪的运行时无法加入该轮，但如果策略允许动态轮次快照，它们可以被选入后续轮次。如果所选运行时数量低于法定数量，系统会记录参与者选择失败事件，以便操作员修复运行时或数据集的就绪状态并重试选择。 | 已翻译 |

- Context: Training Orchestration

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |

| event | 事件 | 已翻译 |
| Operators can retry participant selection after federation members, runtime identities, runtime connectivity, or dataset metadata readiness change. Retry records an explicit request and reuses the normal SelectTrainingRoundParticipants policy instead of duplicating participant selection rules. | 在联邦成员、运行时身份、运行时连接性或数据集元数据就绪状态发生变化后，操作员可以重试参与者选择。重试会记录一个显式请求，并复用常规的 SelectTrainingRoundParticipants 策略，而不是复制参与者选择规则。 | 已翻译 |
| Retry Participant Selection | 重试参与者选择 | 已翻译 |

<!-- em:section id="model-i18n.slice.slice_domain_FederationLearningPlatform_context_TrainingOrchestration_slice_GenerateParticipantExecutionPlan" source="domain/FederationLearningPlatform/context/TrainingOrchestration/slice/GenerateParticipantExecutionPlan" -->
## Slice 术语: Generate Participant Execution Plan

- Context: Training Orchestration

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A participant execution plan must carry the immutable base model artifact snapshot needed by the runtime agent: model identity, pullable artifact URI, registry reference, strict model plugin, model format, artifact digest, and optional signature URI. It must also carry the selected RuntimeEngineProfile snapshot: plugin profile, pullable runtime-engine image, and optional image digest. Runtime agents must not infer or query these values later from mutable repository views, display names, registry references, or local environment variables. Round one uses the initial model snapshot from the training run configuration; later rounds use the aggregated model snapshot recorded when the previous training round completed. | 参与者执行计划必须携带运行时代理所需要的不可变基础模型构件快照：模型标识、可拉取构件 URI、注册表引用、严格模型插件、模型格式、构件摘要以及可选签名 URI。它还必须携带所选的 RuntimeEngineProfile 快照：插件配置文件、可拉取的运行时引擎镜像以及可选镜像摘要。运行时代理不得稍后从可变仓库视图、显示名称、注册表引用或本地环境变量推断或查询这些值。第一轮使用训练运行配置中的初始模型快照；后续轮次使用上一训练轮次完成时记录的聚合模型快照。 | 已翻译 |

<!-- em:section id="model-i18n.slice.slice_domain_FederationLearningPlatform_context_TrainingOrchestration_slice_EvaluateModelUpdateSubmission" source="domain/FederationLearningPlatform/context/TrainingOrchestration/slice/EvaluateModelUpdateSubmission" -->
## Slice 术语: Evaluate Model Update Submission

- Context: Training Orchestration

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A runtime agent submits only the protected model update artifact for secure aggregation rounds. The platform evaluates the submitted metadata, digest, protection type, participant identity, and anomaly signal without receiving plaintext model parameters. ModelUpdateSubmissionAccepted carries the cumulative accepted runtime snapshot for the round so downstream secure aggregation automation can count encrypted updates and complete aggregation. Anomalous update rejection remains an explicit business rule and should be wired to ModelUpdateSubmissionRejected after the anomaly scoring policy is modeled as a conditional decision or port-backed adapter. | 运行时代理仅为安全聚合轮次提交受保护的模型更新制品。平台评估提交的元数据、摘要、保护类型、参与者身份和异常信号，而不接收明文模型参数。ModelUpdateSubmissionAccepted 携带本轮的累计已接受运行时快照，以便下游安全聚合自动化能够统计加密更新数量并完成聚合。异常更新拒绝仍然是一条明确的业务规则，应在异常评分策略建模为条件决策或由端口支持的适配器之后，连接到 ModelUpdateSubmissionRejected。 | 已翻译 |
| Accept Normal Update | 接受正常更新 | 已翻译 |
| Accepted Model Update Artifact Refs | 已接受模型更新工件引用 | 已翻译 |
| Accepted Model Update Artifact Refs is required | 已接受模型更新工件引用为必填项 | 已翻译 |
| Accepted Model Update Count | 已接受的模型更新数量 | 已翻译 |
| Accepted Model Update Count is required | 已接受的模型更新数量为必填项 | 已翻译 |
| Accepted Runtime Ids | 已接受的运行时 ID | 已翻译 |

<!-- em:section id="model-i18n.slice.slice_domain_FederationLearningPlatform_context_TrainingOrchestration_slice_AggregatePlainModelUpdates" source="domain/FederationLearningPlatform/context/TrainingOrchestration/slice/AggregatePlainModelUpdates" -->
## Slice 术语: Aggregate Plain Model Updates

- Context: Training Orchestration

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A round that does not require secure aggregation must aggregate accepted plaintext model updates after every selected runtime has contributed an accepted update. The aggregation input is the cumulative immutable artifact reference snapshot carried by ModelUpdateSubmissionAccepted. Completion produces the same global model metadata required by evaluation and model registration without creating a secure aggregation session. Duplicate submissions from an already accepted runtime must not trigger aggregation again. | 不需要安全聚合的轮次必须在每个所选运行时都贡献了已接受的更新后，聚合已接受的明文模型更新。聚合输入是由 ModelUpdateSubmissionAccepted 携带的累计不可变工件引用快照。完成时生成评估和模型注册所需的相同全局模型元数据，但不创建安全聚合会话。已接受运行时的重复提交不得再次触发聚合。 | 已翻译 |

## Slice 术语: Declare Dataset

- Context: Runtime Agent Operations

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Data Owner | 数据所有者 | 已翻译 |
| Dataset Declared | 数据集已声明 | 已翻译 |
| Dataset Id | 数据集 ID | 已翻译 |
| Dataset Id is required | 数据集 ID 为必填项 | 已翻译 |

| Declare Dataset | 声明数据集 | 已翻译 |
| Declare Dataset With Feature Schema Snapshot | 使用特征架构快照声明数据集 | 已翻译 |
| Declaring a dataset synchronously loads the referenced feature schema through the DeclareDataset port adapter before the decision emits DatasetDeclared. The adapter must enrich the declaration result with the feature and label snapshot so the runtime agent can validate and profile local data without querying the platform later. Dataset usage describes the governance purpose of the dataset, such as training, validation, evaluation, or testing. If the feature schema cannot be loaded, the adapter throws an exception and the declaration does not emit DatasetDeclared. | 声明数据集会通过 DeclareDataset 端口适配器同步加载所引用的特征架构，然后决策才会发出 DatasetDeclared。适配器必须使用特征和标签快照丰富声明结果，以便运行时代理能够验证和分析本地数据，而无需稍后查询平台。数据集用途描述了数据集的治理目的，例如训练、验证、评估或测试。如果无法加载特征架构，适配器将抛出异常，并且声明不会发出 DatasetDeclared。 | 已翻译 |

<!-- em:section id="model-i18n.slice.slice_domain_FederationLearningPlatform_context_RuntimeAgentOperations_slice_ConfigureRuntimeDatasetBinding" source="domain/FederationLearningPlatform/context/RuntimeAgentOperations/slice/ConfigureRuntimeDatasetBinding" -->
## Slice 术语: Configure Runtime Dataset Binding

- Context: Runtime Agent Operations

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A runtime dataset binding represents the current local disk path for one dataset on one runtime. The runtime agent reads the dataset from filePath using dataFormat; database, URL, object storage, and credential-backed sources are not modeled for this binding flow. The same dataset may be bound on different runtimes, and the same runtime may bind different datasets, but one runtime cannot configure multiple current bindings for the same dataset. Reconfiguration should use an explicit reconfigure/update flow instead of creating another binding for the same runtimeId and datasetId. | 运行时数据集绑定表示一个运行时上一个数据集的当前本地磁盘路径。运行时代理使用 dataFormat 从 filePath 读取数据集；此绑定流程未对数据库、URL、对象存储和凭据支持的来源进行建模。同一数据集可以绑定到不同的运行时，同一运行时也可以绑定不同的数据集，但一个运行时不能为同一数据集配置多个当前绑定。重新配置应使用显式的重新配置/更新流程，而不是为同一 runtimeId 和 datasetId 创建另一个绑定。 | 已翻译 |

| Training Run Configuration Id | 训练运行配置 ID | 已翻译 |

| When a participant execution plan is received, the runtime agent performs a local readiness check before starting any training work. The check verifies that the plan targets this runtime, the required dataset binding exists, dataset access has already been validated, the base model is available, the requested training configuration is supported, sufficient local resources are available, and the runtime agent is idle. The port records exactly one outcome: ExecutionPlanAccepted when all local requirements are satisfied, or ExecutionPlanRejected with rejection reasons when any requirement fails. | 当收到参与者执行计划时，运行时代理在开始任何训练工作之前执行本地就绪检查。该检查验证计划针对此运行时、所需的数据集绑定存在、数据集访问已经过验证、基础模型可用、所请求的训练配置受支持、本地资源充足，并且运行时代理处于空闲状态。该端口仅记录一种结果：当所有本地要求均已满足时为 ExecutionPlanAccepted（执行计划已接受），或者当任何要求失败时为 ExecutionPlanRejected（执行计划已拒绝）并附上拒绝原因。 | 已翻译 |

| Start Round Execution | 开始轮次执行 | 已翻译 |
| Start Round Execution When Plan Accepted | 计划接受时启动轮次执行 | 已翻译 |
| Starting a round execution is responsible for launching the local runtime engine on demand for this participant round, not for calling a pre-existing always-on engine. The adapter prepares the local training workspace and configuration, starts the matching runtime engine service, process, or container, submits the local model training job to that engine, and records the created runtimeEngineJobId. The port records exactly one outcome: RoundExecutionStarted when the runtime engine is running and the training job has been accepted, or RoundExecutionStartFailed when environment preparation, engine startup, model loading, resource allocation, or training job submission fails.<br>Runtime engine model selection must come from the participant execution plan baseModelPlugin snapshot. Runtime agents must not decide the model plugin from model display names, registry references, or local environment variables because the selected model is platform training configuration data.<br>Runtime engine image selection must come from the participant execution plan RuntimeEngineProfile snapshot. Runtime-agent local configuration is only a fallback for legacy plans that do not carry a runtimeEngineImage.<br>When secureAggregationRequired is true, StartRoundExecution must pass encryptionScheme, publicKeyVersion, publicKeyRef, and encryptedParameterScale to the runtime engine so the local model update is encrypted inside the participant runtime boundary before any update artifact is reported to the platform. | 启动轮次执行负责按需为该参与者轮次启动本地运行时引擎，而不是调用预先存在的常驻引擎。适配器准备本地训练工作区和配置，启动匹配的运行时引擎服务、进程或容器，向该引擎提交本地模型训练任务，并记录已创建的 runtimeEngineJobId。该端口仅记录一种结果：当运行时引擎正在运行且训练任务已被接受时为 RoundExecutionStarted（轮次执行已启动），或者当环境准备、引擎启动、模型加载、资源分配或训练任务提交失败时为 RoundExecutionStartFailed（轮次执行启动失败）。<br>运行时引擎模型选择必须来自参与者执行计划的 baseModelPlugin 快照。运行时代理不得根据模型显示名称、注册表引用或本地环境变量来决定模型插件，因为所选模型是平台训练配置数据。<br>运行时引擎镜像选择必须来自参与者执行计划的 RuntimeEngineProfile 快照。runtime-agent 本地配置仅是不携带 runtimeEngineImage 的遗留计划的回退方案。<br>当 secureAggregationRequired 为真时，StartRoundExecution 必须将 encryptionScheme、publicKeyVersion、publicKeyRef 和 encryptedParameterScale 传递给运行时引擎，以便在任何更新工件上报至平台之前，本地模型更新在参与者运行时边界内被加密。 | 已翻译 |
| Training Job Id | 训练作业 ID | 已翻译 |
| Training Job Id is required | 训练作业 ID 为必填项 | 已翻译 |
| Training Run Configuration Id | 训练运行配置 ID | 已翻译 |
| Training Run Configuration Id is required | 训练运行配置 ID 为必填项 | 已翻译 |

| Observed Status | 观察到的状态 | 已翻译 |
| Observed Status is required | 观察到的状态为必填项 | 已翻译 |
| Observing a runtime engine job is an external status check and must not advance or reset the RoundExecution lifecycle state. The adapter queries the runtime engine by runtimeEngineJobId and records RuntimeEngineJobObserved with observedStatus RUNNING, COMPLETED, or FAILED. Completion and failure domain state changes are triggered by follow-up automations instead of embedding runtime engine polling inside CompleteRoundExecution or FailRoundExecution.<br>When secure aggregation is required, the completed runtime engine observation must report an encrypted update artifact and digest. The platform must not receive or derive a plaintext local model update artifact for secure aggregation rounds. | 观察运行时引擎作业是一项外部状态检查，不得推进或重置 RoundExecution 的生命周期状态。适配器通过 runtimeEngineJobId 查询运行时引擎，并记录带有 observedStatus 为 RUNNING、COMPLETED 或 FAILED 的 RuntimeEngineJobObserved。完成和失败的领域状态变更由后续自动化触发，而不是在 CompleteRoundExecution 或 FailRoundExecution 中嵌入运行时引擎轮询。<br>当需要安全聚合时，已完成的运行时引擎观察必须报告加密的更新制品和摘要。对于安全聚合轮次，平台不得接收或推导明文本地模型更新制品。 | 已翻译 |

| Runtime agent startup begins by loading bootstrap configuration from environment variables, mounted files, or platform-issued startup material. The configuration must provide the stable runtimeAgentId generated when RuntimeInfrastructure was registered, the matching runtimeInfrastructureId, and the agent version. A successful load starts the runtime-agent lifecycle; a missing or invalid configuration records a bootstrap failure and must not create a new random runtimeAgentId. | 运行时代理启动通过从环境变量、已挂载文件或平台颁发的启动材料加载引导配置来开始。该配置必须提供在 RuntimeInfrastructure 注册时生成的稳定 runtimeAgentId、与之匹配的 runtimeInfrastructureId，以及代理版本。成功加载将启动运行时代理生命周期；缺失或无效的配置会记录引导失败，并且不得创建新的随机 runtimeAgentId。 | 已翻译 |

<!-- em:section id="model-i18n.slice.slice_domain_FederationLearningPlatform_context_RuntimeAgentOperations_slice_ReportRuntimeInstanceConnected" source="domain/FederationLearningPlatform/context/RuntimeAgentOperations/slice/ReportRuntimeInstanceConnected" -->
## Slice 术语: Report Runtime Instance Connected

- Context: Runtime Agent Operations

| 原文 | 译文 | 状态 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| After the runtime instance self-check passes, the runtime agent reports its connection status to the platform by calling POST /runtimeinfrastructure/recordruntimeconnectionestablished. The platform records RuntimeConnectionEstablished only after that command succeeds. The local agent must record AgentRuntimeConnectionReportFailed when the platform API is unreachable, authentication fails, the control channel cannot be established, the heartbeat is rejected, or the platform command is rejected. | 在运行时实例自检通过后，运行时代理通过调用 POST /runtimeinfrastructure/recordruntimeconnectionestablished 向平台报告其连接状态。平台仅在该命令成功后才记录 RuntimeConnectionEstablished。当平台 API 不可达、身份验证失败、控制通道无法建立、心跳被拒绝或平台命令被拒绝时，本地代理必须记录 AgentRuntimeConnectionReportFailed。 | 已翻译 |
